definition{
  name: clause0,
  parameters: [],
  result: Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::Clause,
  body: (record Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::Clause {index := 0, convention := 0, number := "1", statement := "Software using Semantic Versioning MUST declare a public API. This API could be declared in the code itself or exist strictly in documentation. However it is done, it SHOULD be precise and comprehensive.", check := (ctor Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::ClauseCheck.undecidable), how := "states what a release means or contains; not decidable from a tag or a manifest"})
}

definition{
  name: clause1,
  parameters: [],
  result: Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::Clause,
  body: (record Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::Clause {index := 1, convention := 0, number := "2", statement := "A normal version number MUST take the form X.Y.Z where X, Y, and Z are non-negative integers, and MUST NOT contain leading zeroes. X is the major version, Y is the minor version, and Z is the patch version. Each element MUST increase num...", check := (ctor Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::ClauseCheck.checked), how := "the FAQ regular expression on every candidate version"})
}

definition{
  name: clause2,
  parameters: [],
  result: Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::Clause,
  body: (record Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::Clause {index := 2, convention := 0, number := "3", statement := "Once a versioned package has been released, the contents of that version MUST NOT be modified. Any modifications MUST be released as a new version.", check := (ctor Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::ClauseCheck.undecidable), how := "states what a release means or contains; not decidable from a tag or a manifest"})
}

definition{
  name: clause3,
  parameters: [],
  result: Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::Clause,
  body: (record Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::Clause {index := 3, convention := 0, number := "4", statement := "Major version zero (0.y.z) is for initial development. Anything MAY change at any time. The public API SHOULD NOT be considered stable.", check := (ctor Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::ClauseCheck.formless), how := "any 0.y.z is allowed; nothing a version string says can break it"})
}

definition{
  name: clause4,
  parameters: [],
  result: Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::Clause,
  body: (record Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::Clause {index := 4, convention := 0, number := "5", statement := "Version 1.0.0 defines the public API. The way in which the version number is incremented after this release is dependent on this public API and how it changes.", check := (ctor Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::ClauseCheck.undecidable), how := "states what a release means or contains; not decidable from a tag or a manifest"})
}

definition{
  name: clause5,
  parameters: [],
  result: Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::Clause,
  body: (record Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::Clause {index := 5, convention := 0, number := "6", statement := "Patch version Z (x.y.Z | x > 0) MUST be incremented if only backward compatible bug fixes are introduced. A bug fix is defined as an internal change that fixes incorrect behavior.", check := (ctor Foundation.Conventions.V1.Model::ClauseCheck.undecidable), how := "states what a release means or contains; not decidable from a tag or a manifest"})
}

definition{
  name: clause6,
